REWORDED Invariant 4's `low_water_mark` clause and EC-010 to state the constraint as \"derived from config `N`, never a hardcoded fallback constant; derivation MAY be materialized at config-load time OR deferred to first-use/resolution — timing is an implementation choice, not a contract obligation.\" No change to the numeric default (`floor(N/2)`), to the fail-loud validation constraint (`0 <= low_water_mark < N`), to any Postcondition, or to any other Edge Case/Canonical Test Vector/VP. The sanctioned implementation was already conformant under the corrected wording; no code or test change required.
